bools_of_nat_aux = fix bools_of_nat_aux (len x : nat) (acc : list bool) {struct len} : list bool := match len return list bool with
                                                                                                    | O => acc
                                                                                                    | S len' => bools_of_nat_aux len' (Nat.div x (S (S O))) (@cons bool match Nat.eqb (Nat.modulo x (S (S O))) (S O) return bool with
                                                                                                                                                                        | true => true
                                                                                                                                                                        | false => false
                                                                                                                                                                        end acc)
                                                                                                    end
     : forall (_ : nat) (_ : nat) (_ : list bool), list bool

Arguments bools_of_nat_aux (len x)%_nat_scope acc%_list_scope
bools_of_nat = fun (len : Z) (n : nat) => bools_of_nat_aux (Z.to_nat len) n (@nil bool)
     : forall (_ : Z) (_ : nat), list bool

Arguments bools_of_nat len%_Z_scope n%_nat_scope
nat_of_bools_aux = fix nat_of_bools_aux (acc : nat) (bs : list bool) {struct bs} : nat := match bs return nat with
                                                                                          | @nil _ => acc
                                                                                          | @cons _ b bs0 => match b return nat with
                                                                                                             | true => nat_of_bools_aux (Init.Nat.add (Init.Nat.mul (S (S O)) acc) (S O)) bs0
                                                                                                             | false => nat_of_bools_aux (Init.Nat.mul (S (S O)) acc) bs0
                                                                                                             end
                                                                                          end
     : forall (_ : nat) (_ : list bool), nat

Arguments nat_of_bools_aux acc%_nat_scope bs%_list_scope
nat_of_bools = fun bs : list bool => nat_of_bools_aux O bs
     : forall _ : list bool, nat

Arguments nat_of_bools bs%_list_scope
unsigned_of_bools = fun bs : list bool => Z.of_nat (nat_of_bools bs)
     : forall _ : list bool, Z

Arguments unsigned_of_bools bs%_list_scope
signed_of_bools = fun bs : list bool => match bs return Z with
                                        | @nil _ => Z0
                                        | @cons _ y _ => match y return Z with
                                                         | true => Z.sub Z0 (Z.add (Zpos xH) (unsigned_of_bools (@map bool bool negb bs)))
                                                         | false => unsigned_of_bools bs
                                                         end
                                        end
     : forall _ : list bool, Z

Arguments signed_of_bools bs%_list_scope
int_of_bools = fun (sign : bool) (bs : list bool) => match sign return Z with
                                                     | true => signed_of_bools bs
                                                     | false => unsigned_of_bools bs
                                                     end
     : forall (_ : bool) (_ : list bool), Z

Inductive ChooseType : Set :=  ChooseBool : ChooseType | ChooseInt : ChooseType | ChooseNat : ChooseType | ChooseReal : ChooseType | ChooseString : ChooseType | ChooseRange : forall (_ : Z) (_ : Z), ChooseType | ChooseBitvector : forall _ : Z, ChooseType.

choose_type = fun ty : ChooseType => match ty return Set with
                                     | ChooseBool => bool
                                     | ChooseInt => Z
                                     | ChooseNat => Z
                                     | ChooseReal => ConstructiveCauchyReals.CReal
                                     | ChooseString => string
                                     | ChooseRange _ _ => Z
                                     | ChooseBitvector n => mword n
                                     end
     : forall _ : ChooseType, Set

Arguments choose_type ty
choose_prop = fun ty : ChooseType => match ty as ty0 return (forall _ : choose_type ty0, Prop) with
                                     | ChooseBool => fun _ : choose_type ChooseBool => True
                                     | ChooseInt => fun _ : choose_type ChooseInt => True
                                     | ChooseNat => fun _ : choose_type ChooseNat => True
                                     | ChooseReal => fun _ : choose_type ChooseReal => True
                                     | ChooseString => fun _ : choose_type ChooseString => True
                                     | ChooseRange lo hi => fun z : choose_type (ChooseRange lo hi) => and (Z.le lo z) (Z.le z hi)
                                     | ChooseBitvector _n => fun _ : choose_type (ChooseBitvector _n) => True
                                     end
     : forall (ty : ChooseType) (_ : choose_type ty), Prop
